  259 |     test('TC-009: Duplicate check case sensitivity matches product behavior', async ({
  260 |       page,
  261 |       trackProgram,

  264 |       const lowercaseVariant = programName.toLowerCase();
  265 |       const programsPage = await createProgramViaUi(
